Mathlib.Algebra.Category.MonCat.Limits

AddCommMonCat.limitConedefMathlib.Algebra.Category.MonCat.Limits, function or constant
Wording
the limit cone of F (from the name)
English
Let $F$ be a functor of $J$ and the set of additive commutative monoid categories. Then the limit cone of $F$ is a cone of $F$ defined as the lift limit of $\operatorname{isLimit}$ applied to the composition of $F$ and the forgetful functor of the set of additive commutative monoid categories and the set of additive monoid categories.
Formula
$F \in \operatorname{Functor} (J , \mathrm{AddCommMonCat})\vdash \operatorname{limitCone} (F):= \operatorname{liftLimit} (\operatorname{isLimit} (\operatorname{comp} (F , \mathrm{CategoryTheory\_\_forget\_u2082\_} (\mathrm{AddCommMonCat} , \mathrm{AddMonCat}))))\in \operatorname{Cone} (F)$

Wording

English

Then the chosen cone of $F$ is an element of $\operatorname{IsLimit}$ applied to the limit cone of $F$ defined as the lifted cone of $\operatorname{isLimit}$ applied to the composition of $F$ and the forgetful functor of the set of additive commutative monoid categories and the set of additive monoid categories.

AddMonCat.limitπAddMonoidHomdefMathlib.Algebra.Category.MonCat.Limits, function or constant
Wording
the limit projection additive monoid homomorphism of F and j (from the name)
English
Let $F$ be a functor of $J$ and the set of additive monoid categories. Let $j$ be an element of $J$. Then the limit projection additive monoid homomorphism of $F$ and $j$ is an additive monoid homomorphism of $\operatorname{pt}$ applied to the limit cone of the composition of $F$ and the forgetful functor of the set of additive monoid categories and the set of carriers of the action of $F$ and $j$.
Formula
$F \in \operatorname{Functor} (J , \mathrm{AddMonCat}), j \in J \vdash \mathrm{AddMonCat\_\_limit\_u3c0\_AddMonoidHom} (F , j)\in \mathrm{AddMonoidHom} (\operatorname{pt} (\operatorname{limitCone} (\operatorname{comp} (F , \operatorname{forget} (\mathrm{AddMonCat})))), \operatorname{carrier} (\operatorname{obj} (F , j)))$
AddMonCat.sectionsAddSubmonoiddefMathlib.Algebra.Category.MonCat.Limits, function or constant
Wording
the section additive submonoid of F (from the name)
English
Let $F$ be a functor of $J$ and the set of additive monoid categories. Then the section additive submonoid of $F$ is an additive submonoid of the set of functions from elements $j$ of $J$ to carriers of the action of $F$ and $j$.
Formula
$F \in \operatorname{Functor} (J , \mathrm{AddMonCat})\vdash \operatorname{sectionsAddSubmonoid} (F)\in \mathrm{AddSubmonoid} (J \rightarrow \operatorname{carrier} (\operatorname{obj} (F , j)))$

Wording

English

Wording
the Coyoneda adjoint of R (from the name)
English
Let $R$ be a category of commutative rings. Then the Coyoneda adjoint of $R$ is an adjunction of the right opposite of the action of the flip of the Coyoneda and $R$ and the action of the Yoneda and $R$.
Formula
$R \in \mathrm{CommRingCat} \vdash \operatorname{coyonedaAdj} (R)\in \operatorname{Adjunction} (\operatorname{rightOp} (\operatorname{obj} (\operatorname{flip} (\operatorname{coyoneda}), R)), \operatorname{obj} (\operatorname{yoneda} , R))$

CategoryTheory.Iso.commRingCatIsoToRingEquivdefMathlib.Algebra.Category.Ring.Basic, function or constant
Wording
the commutative ring category isomorphism to ring isomorphism of e (from the name)
English
Let $e$ be an isomorphism of $R$ and $S$. Then the commutative ring category isomorphism to ring isomorphism of $e$ is a ring isomorphism of the set of carriers of $R$ and the set of carriers of $S$.
Formula

CommRingCat.Hom.homdefMathlib.Algebra.Category.Ring.Basic, function or constant
Wording
the underlying ring hom of f (from the docstring)
English
Let $f$ be a homomorphism of $R$ and $S$. Then the underlying ring hom of $f$ is a ring homomorphism of the set of carriers of $R$ and the set of carriers of $S$ defined as the homomorphism of $f$.
Formula
$f \in \operatorname{Hom} (R , S)\vdash \operatorname{hom} (f):= \operatorname{hom} (f)\in \mathrm{RingHom} (\operatorname{carrier} (R), \operatorname{carrier} (S))$
CommRingCat.Hom.hom'defMathlib.Algebra.Category.Ring.Basic, function or constant
Wording
the underlying ring hom of self (from the docstring)
English
Let $\mathrm{self}$ be a homomorphism of $R$ and $S$. Then the underlying ring hom of $\mathrm{self}$ is a ring homomorphism of the set of carriers of $R$ and the set of carriers of $S$ defined as $\operatorname{proj0}$ applied to $\mathrm{self}$.
Formula
$\mathrm{self} \in \operatorname{Hom} (R , S)\vdash \operatorname{hom'} (\mathrm{self}):= \operatorname{proj0} (\mathrm{self})\in \mathrm{RingHom} (\operatorname{carrier} (R), \operatorname{carrier} (S))$
CommRingCat.carrierdefMathlib.Algebra.Category.Ring.Basic, type
Wording
carrier of self (from the name)
English
Let $\mathrm{self}$ be a category of commutative rings. Then a carrier of $\mathrm{self}$ is an element of the function that maps $\mathrm{self}$ to $\operatorname{proj0}$ applied to $\mathrm{self}$.
Formula
$\mathrm{self} \in \mathrm{CommRingCat} \vdash \operatorname{carrier} (\mathrm{self}):= \lambda \mathrm{self} . \operatorname{proj0} (\mathrm{self})$
CommRingCat.forget_obj_eq_coedefMathlib.Algebra.Category.Ring.Basic, predicate
Wording
no wording
English
Let $R$ and $\mathrm{R'}$ be categories of commutative rings. Then $\mathrm{CommRingCat\_\_forget\_obj\_eq\_coe}$ holds for $R$ and $\mathrm{R'}$, if if $\EqtMACRoH {R}{\mathrm{R'}}$, then the action of the forgetful functor of the set of categories of commutative rings and $R$ is equal to the set of carriers of $\mathrm{R'}$.
Formula
$R , \mathrm{R'} \in \mathrm{CommRingCat} \vdash \mathrm{CommRingCat\_\_forget\_obj\_eq\_coe} (R , \mathrm{R'}):\Leftrightarrow \EqtMACRoJ {R}{\mathrm{R'}}\rightarrow \EqtMACRoJ {\operatorname{obj} (\operatorname{forget} (\mathrm{CommRingCat}), R)}{\operatorname{carrier} (\mathrm{R'})}$

Let $f$ be a homomorphism of $X$ and $Y$. Then the homomorphism of $f$ is a multiplicative homomorphism of the set of carriers of $X$ and the set of carriers of $Y$ defined as the homomorphism of $f$.
Formula
$f \in \operatorname{Hom} (X , Y)\vdash \operatorname{hom} (f):= \operatorname{hom} (f)\in \mathrm{MulHom} (\operatorname{carrier} (X), \operatorname{carrier} (Y))$
Semigrp.Hom.hom'defMathlib.Algebra.Category.Semigrp.Basic, function or constant
Wording
the homomorphism of self (from the name)
English
Let $\mathrm{self}$ be a homomorphism of $A$ and $B$. Then the homomorphism of $\mathrm{self}$ is a multiplicative homomorphism of the set of carriers of $A$ and the set of carriers of $B$ defined as $\operatorname{proj0}$ applied to $\mathrm{self}$.
Formula
$\mathrm{self} \in \operatorname{Hom} (A , B)\vdash \operatorname{hom'} (\mathrm{self}):= \operatorname{proj0} (\mathrm{self})\in \mathrm{MulHom} (\operatorname{carrier} (A), \operatorname{carrier} (B))$
Semigrp.carrierdefMathlib.Algebra.Category.Semigrp.Basic, type
Wording
carrier of self (from the name)
English
Let $\mathrm{self}$ be a semigroup. Then a carrier of $\mathrm{self}$ is an element of the function that maps $\mathrm{self}$ to $\operatorname{proj0}$ applied to $\mathrm{self}$.
Formula
$\mathrm{self} \in \mathrm{Semigrp} \vdash \operatorname{carrier} (\mathrm{self}):= \lambda \mathrm{self} . \operatorname{proj0} (\mathrm{self})$

English

IsPrimaldefMathlib.Algebra.Divisibility.Basic, predicate
Wording
a is primal (by hand)
English
Let $a$ be an element of $x$. Then $a$ is primal, if for all elements $b$ and $c$ of $x$, if $a$ divides $b \times c$, then there exist elements $x_{2}$ and $x_{3}$ of $x$, such that $x_{2}$ divides $b$, $x_{3}$ divides $c$ and $\EqtMACRoH {a}{x_{2} \times x_{3}}$.
Formula
$a \in x \vdash \mathrm{IsPrimal} (a):\Leftrightarrow \forall b \in x . \forall c \in x . \divides {a}{b \times c}\rightarrow (\exists x_{2} \in x . \exists x_{3} \in x . \divides {x_{2}}{b}\wedge (\divides {x_{3}}{c}\wedge (\EqtMACRoJ {a}{x_{2} \times x_{3}})))$

Mathlib.Algebra.Divisibility.Units

IsRelPrimedefMathlib.Algebra.Divisibility.Units, predicate
Wording
no wording
English
Let $x_{2}$ and $y$ be elements of $x$. Then $\mathrm{IsRelPrime}$ holds for $x_{2}$ and $y$, if for all elements $d$ of $x$, if $d$ divides $x_{2}$, then if $d$ divides $y$, then $d$ is a unit.
Formula
$x_{2} , y \in x \vdash \mathrm{IsRelPrime} (x_{2} , y):\Leftrightarrow \forall d \in x . \divides {d}{x_{2}}\rightarrow \divides {d}{y}\rightarrow \mathrm{IsUnit} (d)$

Mathlib.Algebra.DualNumber

DualNumberdefMathlib.Algebra.DualNumber, type
Wording
dual number of R (from the name)
English
Let $R$ be a set. Then a dual number of $R$ is an element of the function that maps $R$ to the set of trivial square zero extensions of $R$ and $R$.
Formula
$R \in \mathrm{Set} \vdash \mathrm{DualNumber} (R):= \lambda R . \mathrm{TrivSqZeroExt} (R , R)$
DualNumber.epsdefMathlib.Algebra.DualNumber, function or constant
Wording
no wording
English
$\operatorname{eps}$ is a dual number of $R$ defined as the right injection of $1$.
Formula
$\operatorname{eps} := \operatorname{inr} (1)\in \mathrm{DualNumber} (R)$

Wording
the lift (from the name)
English
The lift is an equivalence from $\SubtypeSet {\mathrm{fe}}{\mathrm{and} (\EqtMACRoH {(\ProdsndMACRoAA {\mathrm{fe}}\times \ProdsndMACRoAA {\mathrm{fe}})}{0}, \mathrm{forall} (A , \lambda a . \mathrm{Commute} (\ProdsndMACRoAA {\mathrm{fe}}, \operatorname{fst} (\mathrm{fe} , a))))}$ to the set of algebra homomorphisms of the set of dual numbers of $A$ and $B$.
Formula
$\operatorname{lift} \in \EquivMACRoAL {(\SubtypeSet {\mathrm{fe}}{\mathrm{and} (\EqtMACRoJ {(\ProdsndMACRoAH {\mathrm{fe}}\times \ProdsndMACRoAH {\mathrm{fe}})}{0}, \mathrm{forall} (A , \lambda a . \mathrm{Commute} (\ProdsndMACRoAH {\mathrm{fe}}, \operatorname{fst} (\mathrm{fe} , a))))})}{\mathrm{AlgHom} (R , \mathrm{DualNumber} (A), B)}$

Mathlib.Algebra.DualQuaternion

Quaternion.dualNumberEquivdefMathlib.Algebra.DualQuaternion, function or constant
Wording
the dual number equivalence (from the name)
English
The dual number equivalence is an algebra isomorphism of the set of quaternions of the set of dual numbers of $R$ and the set of dual numbers of the set of quaternions of $R$.
Formula
$\operatorname{dualNumberEquiv} \in \mathrm{AlgEquiv} (R , \mathrm{Quaternion} (\mathrm{DualNumber} (R)), \mathrm{DualNumber} (\mathrm{Quaternion} (R)))$
